Custom filter to sanitize sensitive data\n        class SanitizeFilter(logging.Filter):\n            def filter(self, record):\n                # Remove any potential key patterns from logs\n                msg = record.getMessage()\n                # Don't log anything that looks like a key\n                if any(x in msg.lower() for x in ['key=', 'password=', 'secret=', 'token=']):\n                    record.msg = \"[SANITIZED]\"\n                    record.args = ()\n                return True\n        \n        self.logger.addFilter(SanitizeFilter())\n    \n    def encrypt_env_file(self, env_path: str, password: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Encrypt .env file to .env.vault format.\n        \n        Args:\n            env_path: Path to .env file\n            password: Password for encryption\n            \n        Raises:\n            FileNotFoundError: If .env file doesn't exist\n        \"\"\"\n        env_path = Path(env_path)\n        \n        if not env_path.exists():\n            raise FileNotFoundError(f\"Environment file not found: {env_path}\")\n        \n        # Read .env file\n        with open(env_path, 'r') as f:\n            env_content = f.read()\n        \n        # Derive key from password\n        key = self.key_manager.derive_key(password)\n        \n        # Encrypt content\n        nonce, ciphertext = encrypt_data(env_content.encode('utf-8'), key)\n        \n        # Write vault file\n        with open(self.vault_path, 'w') as f:\n            f.write(VAULT_MAGIC)\n            f.write(base64.b64encode(self.key_manager.salt).decode('utf-8') + '\\n')\n            f.write(base64.b64encode(nonce).decode('utf-8') + '\\n')\n            f.write(base64.b64encode(ciphertext).decode('utf-8') + '\\n')\n        \n        self.logger.info(f\"Encrypted {env_path} to {self.vault_path}\")\n        \n        # Clear key from memory\n        self.key_manager.clear_key()\n    \n    def load(self, password: str) -> Dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Load and decrypt .env.vault to memory.\n        \n        Args:\n            password: Password for decryption\n            \n        Returns:\n            Dictionary of environment variables\n            \n        Raises:\n            FileNotFoundError: If vault file doesn't exist\n            ValueError: If vault format is invalid\n            cryptography.exceptions.InvalidTag: If password is wrong\n        \"\"\"\n        if not self.vault_path.exists():\n            raise FileNotFoundError(f\"Vault file not found: {self.vault_path}\")\n        \n        # Read vault file\n        with open(self.vault_path, 'r') as f:\n            lines = f.read().strip().split('\\n')\n        \n        # Validate format\n        if len(lines) < 4:\n            raise ValueError(\"Invalid vault file format\")\n        \n        if lines[0] != VAULT_HEADER:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Invalid vault header: {lines[0]}\")\n        \n        # Parse vault data\n        salt = base64.b64decode(lines[1])\n        nonce = base64.b64decode(lines[2])\n        ciphertext = base64.b64decode(lines[3])\n        \n        # Derive key from password and salt\n        self.key_manager = KeyManager(custom_salt=salt)\n        key = self.key_manager.derive_key(password)\n        \n        # Decrypt content\n        plaintext = decrypt_data(nonce, ciphertext, key)\n        \n        # Parse environment variables\n        env_content = plaintext.decode('utf-8')\n        self._env_vars = self._parse_env_content(env_content)\n        \n        # Mark as loaded\n        self._loaded = True\n        \n        # Clear key from memory after loading\n        self.key_manager.clear_key()\n        \n        self.logger.info(f\"Loaded {len(self._env_vars)} environment variables from vault\")\n        \n        return self._env_vars\n    \n    def _parse_env_content(self, content: str) -> Dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Parse .env file content to dictionary.\n        \n        Args:\n            content: .env file content\n            \n        Returns:\n            Dictionary of environment variables\n        \"\"\"\n        env_vars = {}\n        \n        for line in content.split('\\n'):\n            line = line.strip()\n            \n            # Skip empty lines and comments\n            if not line or line.startswith('#'):\n                continue\n            \n            # Parse KEY=VALUE\n            if '=' in line:\n                key, value = line.split('=', 1)\n                key = key.strip()\n                value = value.strip()\n                \n                # Remove quotes if present\n                if value.startswith('\"') and value.endswith('\"'):\n                    value = value[1:-1]\n                elif value.startswith(\"'\") and value.endswith(\"'\"):\n                    value = value[1:-1]\n                \n                env_vars[key] = value\n        \n        return env_vars\n    \n    def get(self, key: str, default: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Get environment variable from memory.\n        \n        Args:\n            key: Variable name\n            default: Default value if not found\n            \n        Returns:\n            Variable value or default\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._env_vars.get(key, default)\n    \n    def get_all(self) -> Dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Get all environment variables from memory.\n        \n        Returns:\n            Dictionary of all variables\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._env_vars.copy()\n    \n    def set_env(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Set loaded variables to os.environ.\n        \n        Raises:\n            RuntimeError: If vault not loaded\n        \"\"\"\n        if not self._loaded:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Vault not loaded. Call load() first.\")\n        \n        for key, value in self._env_vars.items():\n            os.environ[key] = value\n        \n        self.logger.info(f\"Set {len(self._env_vars)} environment variables\")\n    \n    def is_loaded(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if vault is loaded.\"\"\"\n        return self._loaded\n    \n    def clear(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Clear all variables from memory.\"\"\"\n        self._env_vars.clear()\n        self._loaded = False\n        self.key_manager.clear_key()\n        self.logger.info(\"Cleared vault from memory\")\n", "old_text": "", "short_description": "Create vault.py main class"}}

Main Vault class for .env management\n\n### Security Features\n\n#### Encryption\n- Algorithm: AES-256-GCM (Galois/Counter Mode)\n- Key derivation: PBKDF2 with 100,000 iterations\n- Nonce: 12 bytes (96 bits) for GCM\n- Key: 32 bytes (256 bits)\n\n#### Key Management\n- Keys derived from machine-specific identifiers (hostname, OS, MAC address)\n- Keys stored in memory only, never written to disk\n- Keys cleared from memory after use\n- Salt generated from system identifiers\n\n#### Log Sanitization\n- Custom logging filter to prevent key leakage\n- Patterns sanitized: `key=`, `password=`, `secret=`, `token=`\n- Sensitive log messages replaced with `[SANITIZED]`\n\n### File Format\n\n```\n.env.vault structure:\n- Line 1: VAULT_V1 (header)\n- Line 2: Salt (base64, 32 bytes)\n- Line 3: Nonce (base64, 12 bytes)\n- Line 4: Encrypted data (base64)\n```\n\n### Usage Pattern\n\n```python\nfrom backend.vault import Vault\n\n# Encrypt .env file\nvault = Vault(vault_path=\".env.vault\")\nvault.encrypt_env_file(\".env\", \"password\")\n\n# Load and decrypt\nvault = Vault(vault_path=\".env.vault\")\nenv_vars = vault.load(\"password\")\n\n# Access variables\napi_key = vault.get(\"API_KEY\")\nvault.set_env()  # Set to os.environ\n```\n\n## Test Results\n\nAll integration tests passed:\n- \u2713 AES-256-GCM encryption working\n- \u2713 Keys loaded to RAM only at startup\n- \u2713 No key leakage to stdout or logs\n- \u2713 System key management implemented\n- \u2713 All unit tests pass\n\n## Dependencies Added\n\n- `cryptography>=41.0.0` - ## ✅ TASK-014 Complete: Local Vault Implementation

Successfully implemented secure .env encryption with AES-256-GCM. All acceptance criteria met.

### 📦 Components Created

**Core Vault Module** (`backend/vault/`):
- `__init__.py` - Module initialization
- `encryption.py` - AES-256-GCM encryption/decryption
- `key_manager.py` - PBKDF2 key derivation from machine identifiers
- `vault.py` - Main Vault class with .env management

**Tests**:
- `tests/test_vault.py` - Comprehensive unit tests
- `scripts/test_vault_integration.py` - Integration test suite

### 🔒 Security Features

✅ **AES-256-GCM Encryption**
- Authenticated encryption with 12-byte nonces
- 32-byte keys derived via PBKDF2 (100k iterations)

✅ **Memory-Only Key Storage**
- Keys never written to disk in plaintext
- Automatically cleared from memory after use

✅ **Log Sanitization**
- Custom filter prevents key leakage to stdout/logs
- Patterns sanitized: `key=`, `password=`, `secret=`, `token=`

✅ **Machine-Specific Keys**
- Derived from hostname, OS, MAC address
- Not portable between machines

### 📄 File Format

```
.env.vault:
VAULT_V1
<salt_base64>
<nonce_base64>
<encrypted_data_base64>
```

### ✅ Test Results

All integration tests passed:
- ✓ AES-256-GCM encryption working
- ✓ Keys loaded to RAM only at startup
- ✓ No key leakage to stdout or logs
- ✓ System key management implemented
- ✓ All unit tests pass

### 📝 Usage Example

```python
from backend.vault import Vault

# Encrypt .env file
vault = Vault()
vault.encrypt_env_file(".env", "password")

# Load and decrypt
vault = Vault()
env_vars = vault.load("password")
api_key = vault.get("API_KEY")
vault.set_env()  # Set to os.environ
```

### 📦 Dependencies Added

- `cryptography>=41.0.0` added to requirements.txt
